Let's design custom projected Adam in detail.

Objective smooth: Φ(h, beta) = (dx/beta) logsumexp(beta * C_unnorm) (C_unnorm array length 2n-1). To avoid overflow, subtract max.
Gradient via weights w = softmax(beta*C_unnorm), grad = dx*(V - S1 - S2) where correlation formula with lag index q? Need ensure orientation with np.correlate h,a. We derived W indexed by l where c_idx m = n-1 + l, c=∑ (1-h_k)h_{k+l}. For derivative: grad = ∑ W_l [I(p-l valid)(1-h_{p-l}) - I(p+l valid) h_{p+l}]. Pseudo for optimize_adam(h,n,time_limit, beta_schedule, steps):
best_h,hard.
for beta in betas:
  for iter in range(maxit_beta):
    c=corr(h)
    hard=max(c)*dx
    if hard<best: update
    weights=softmax(β(c-cmax))
    grad=weighted_grad(weights)
    # Adam update
    m1=β1 m1+(1-β1)grad; v2=...; mhat, vhat
    h_candidate = h - lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    h=project_feasible(h_candidate,n)
    maybe warmup? 
  Optionally reduce lr.
- Return.

def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
 # maybe if min/max already within [0,1] and sum diff small
 target=n/2.0
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 diff=h.sum()-target
 if abs(diff)<1e-12: return h
 # If diff>0, need decrease; if only >1e-15 adjust. If diff<0 increase.
 # Bisection over tau:
 # F(tau)=sum(clip(h-tau,0,1)) - target. decreasing.
 # We need find tau such that F=0. Since h in [0,1], tau in [-1,1]. But if target near sum? Use lo=-1.1, hi=1.1.
 # However h may all 0? target 0 impossible? n/2 within [0,n]. ok.
 # 40 iterations, vector clip.
 for _ in range(45): mid=(lo+hi)/2; f=(np.clip(h-mid,0,1).sum()-target); if f>0: lo=mid else hi=mid.
 h=np.clip(h-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
 # correct residual by adjusting a non-bound value:
 residual=h.sum()-target
 if abs(residual)>1e-12:
   idxs? choose first with 0<h<1. adjustment = residual/(number interior). If none, maybe use np.where.
Better after bisection residual tiny. Add correction:
 interior=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
 if interior.sum()>0: h[interior]-= residual/interior.sum(); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
Return.

Faster projection using water-filling with sorting: A standard projection onto sum and box:
Given x, need τ such that sum(clip(x-τ,0,1))=t. Instead of bisection 45*n, we can do O(n log n) sort. But n=1000 sort each step 5000 =>5e6 log, maybe okay. Bisection vectorized in C (clip) faster for moderate n. Use bisection 35 iterations.

We can add quick fix: If sum diff small (<1e-6), adjust interior uniformly instead of full bisection. During Adam, projection from small step near feasible may have sum slightly off; uniform adjust and clip enough. But after clip sum can change. Use iterative few times. Function:
def project_feasible_fast(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1); target=n/2
 for it in range(5):
   diff=h.sum()-target
   if abs(diff)<1e-10: break
   mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
   if mask.sum()==0: break
   h[mask]-=diff/mask.sum()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # If sum not exact, bisection
 if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-8: bisection...
 return h.
This is faster.

Projection:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target=float(n)/2.0
    # clip
    if h.size != n: h=np.resize? not.
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # quick uniform iterations
    for _ in range(6):
       diff=h.sum()-target
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
       if mask.sum()==0: break
       h[mask]-=diff/mask.sum()
       h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-9:
       lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
       # Ensure F(lo)>0? if h-lo clip: if lo=-1, clip(h+1,0,1) >=h maybe sum≥target? For target within [0,n], okay. hi=1, sum clip(h-1)=0 <=target.
       for _ in range(50):
          mid=(lo+hi)/2
          s=np.clip(h-mid,0,1).sum()
          if s>target: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid
       tau=(lo+hi)*0.5
       h=np.clip(h-tau,0,1)
       res=h.sum()-target
       if abs(res)>1e-12:
          mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
          if mask.sum()>0:
             h[mask]-=res/mask.sum()
             h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This maintains. If h input has nan, fix? maybe.

def compute_c_values(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
def compute_c5(h,n): c=compute_c_values(h); return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
```

Soft weights:
```
def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    m=np.max(c)
    x=beta*(c-m)
    # for beta=0 return uniform
    if beta <=0: return np.full_like(c,1.0/len(c))
    x=x - np.max(x) (already maybe 0)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0:
      w=np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)]=1; return w
    w/=s; return w
```
For β very large, exp underflow; subtract max.

Weighted gradient:
```
def weighted_gradient(w,h,n):
    # w length 2n-1
    # prefix V
    pref=np.zeros(2*n)
    np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:]) ? top-level allowed? Use np.cumsum
    V = pref[n:n+n] - pref[0:n] ? Wait pref indices: pref[0]=0, pref[i+1]=sum w[0:i+1]. Sum q=p to p+n-1 = pref[p+n]-pref[p]. So V = pref[n:2n] - pref[0:n]. (for p=0..n-1)
    conv1=np.convolve(h,w,mode='full')
    S1=conv1[n-1:n-1+n]
    wrev=w[::-1]
    conv2=np.convolve(h,wrev,mode='full')
    S2=conv2[n-1:n-1+n]
    grad=V - S1 - S2
    return grad
```
Check V via pref: p+n max n-1+n-1=2n-2, pref index up to 2n-1, V length n. yes.

Smooth objective:
```
def soft_objective(h,n,beta):
    c=compute_c_values(h)
    m=np.max(c)
    if beta<=0: return m
    return (m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(beta*(c-m))))) / beta
```
Fun for SLSQP with args (n,beta). It computes c each time. Grad recompute c; okay.

Hard objective and grad:
```
def hard_objective(h,n): return float(np.max(compute_c_values(h))) # unnormalized? For SLSQP maximizing? We minimize max c (without dx) as dx constant. Use c max. Return max c.
def hard_gradient(h,n): c=compute; idx=np.argmax; w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return weighted_gradient(w,h,n)
```
Use unnormalized c for optimization; C = maxc dx.

Run a SLSQP optimizer:
```
def run_slsqp(h,n,beta,maxiter,time_left):
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n; cons=[...]
   try:
     if beta<=0: res=minimize(hard_objective,h,jac=hard_gradient,args=(n),method='SLSQP',bounds,cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
     else: res=minimize(soft_objective,h,jac=soft_gradient,args=(n,beta),...)
   except: return h
   h2=project_feasible(res.x,n); return h2
```
But time inside minimize cannot check; use maxiter limited.

Adam optimizer:
```
def optimize_adam(h,n,time_limit,beta_steps,lr):
    t_end=time.time()+time_limit
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    # maybe run multi-pass beta schedule
    for beta,beta_iters in beta_steps:
       if time.time()>t_end: break
       m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0
       stalled=0
       while t < beta_iters and time.time()<t_end-0.5:
          c=compute_c_values(h)
          hardc=np.max(c)*2.0/n
          if hardc < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=hardc; best_h=h.copy(); stalled=0
          else: stalled+=1
          # if stalled > 200 and t>50: reduce lr? We'll external.
          w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
          grad=weighted_gradient(w,h,n)
          # normalize gradient? To avoid scale issues, use rms
          t +=1
          m1 = 0.9*m1 + 0.1*grad
          v2 = 0.999*v2 + 0.001*grad*grad
          mhat = m1/(1-0.9**t)
          vhat = v2/(1-0.999**t)
          step = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
          # If steps too large? Clip step to max 0.5 maybe
          step = np.clip(step, -0.5, 0.5)
          h = project_feasible(h - lr*step, n)
       # maybe after beta, set h = best_h if no improvement? Usually keep.
    return best_h,best_c
```
This uses Adam with lr maybe 0.05-0.2. Step = normalized grad (~±1). lr 0.05 small. Need enough iterations. Maybe lr 0.1. For high n, moving 0.1 each coordinate too large? But projection. Could adapt.

Maybe lr should scale with 1/np.sqrt(n)? Let's estimate: c changes by ~∑ over active overlaps; if change one h by 0.1, c for that lag changes at most 0.1, total objective units c ~0.1. In C units 0.1*dx. Gradient coordinate ~O(1). Adam step ~lr. To reduce C by 0.001 in C units, need c reduce 0.05 for n=200, coordinate moves of 0.01 maybe. lr 0.05 reasonable. Use multiple passes. Could set lr 0.1 for initial beta, 0.03 final.

But if gradient zeros for many coordinates (inactive), no move. Good.

Potential issue: Adam with projection and equality can destroy sum, but projection preserves. If h becomes saturated many variables, equality still.

def make_binary_pal(n, seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   m=n//2
   if m%2 !=0? For n multiple of4, m even. pair_count ones = n//4.
   idx=np.arange(m); rng.shuffle(idx); ones=idx[:n//4]

Local search:
```
def improve_binary(h,n,budget,seed):
   h=h.copy()
   best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   t_end=time.time()+budget
   ones_pairs, zeros_pairs = get pairs with value1 and 0.
   T=1.0? objective Δ in C units maybe. Scale Δ by n/2? Use Δ_count = maxc - best_mc where maxc unnormalized. Let maxc = ...
   for iter until time:
      if ones_pairs.size==0 or zeros==0: break
      i=ones_pairs[rng.randint(size)]; j=zeros_pairs[...]
      # swap: set i and j zero, mirror? Actually pair index in 0..m-1 corresponds positions p and p2=n-1-p. Swapping pair i (currently1) with j (0): set pair i to0, pair j to1.
      h[i]=0; h[n-1-i]=0; h[j]=1; h[n-1-j]=1
      new_c=compute_c5(h,n)
      if new_c <= best_c or rng.random() < math.exp(-(new_c-best_c)*accept_scale): accept
      else revert
      if accept update best and lists
      update T anneal
```

Need update ones/zeros lists after swap: use sets? For performance, maintain boolean pair values. Simpler: recompute lists every few iterations or maintain with Python lists and remove/add. N small.
```
ones=np.where(pairval==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(pairval==0)[0]

   h0=project_feasible(np.array(initial_h_values,float), n0=len(initial_h_values))

    hh,cc=optimize_adam(best_h,best_n,60,betas=[10,40,150], lr=0.05?)
    consider(hh,best_n)
    if best_n <=200 and remaining>60: hh=slsqp(...)
 # 2. Binary searches at n multiples 4
 budget remains.

 # 4. Random perturbations of best at best_n
 for i in range(num_pert):

   consider
 # 5. Final polish best if n<=150 using SLSQP soft/hard

Let's refine optimize_adam to accept time slice and use beta schedule and lr schedule. We need careful with stopping and best tracking.

```
def adam_refine(h,n,time_slice,beta_list,lr_list):
    t_end=time.time()+time_slice
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    # If time small, do few.
    for bi,beta in enumerate(beta_list):
       if time.time() > t_end: break
       lr = lr_list[min(bi,len(lr_list)-1)]
       m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0; no_improve=0
       max_iter = 100000
       while t < max_iter and time.time() < t_end - 1:
          t+=1
          c=compute_c_values(h)
          hard=float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
          if hard < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_c=hard; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
          else: no_improve+=1
          w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
          grad=weighted_gradient(w,h,n)
          m1 = 0.9*m1 + 0.1*grad
          v2 = 0.999*v2 + 0.001*grad*grad
          b1t=1-0.9**t
          b2t=1-0.999**t
          step=m1/b1t/(np.sqrt(v2/b2t)+1e-8)
          # clip step to [-1,1]
          step=np.clip(step,-1,1)
          h=project_feasible(h - lr*step, n)
          if no_improve > 400 and t > 100:
             # reduce lr for this pass? break to next beta
             lr *=0.5
             # reset no improve
       # after pass, maybe if beta high and no improve break
    return best_h,best_c
```

Now `adam_refine` in detail, with beta list and lr list same length? We'll accept `beta_lr` list of tuples.
```
def adam_refine(h,n,time_limit,beta_lr):
    if time_limit <=0: return h, compute_c5(h,n)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    t_end=time.time()+time_limit
    for beta,lr in beta_lr:
       if time.time()>=t_end-1: break
       m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0; stall=0
       max_iter=1000000
       while t<max_iter and time.time()<t_end-0.5:
          t+=1
          c=compute_c_values(h)
          hard=float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
          if hard < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_c=hard; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
          else:
             stall+=1
          w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
          grad=weighted_gradient(w,h,n)
          # If gradient nonfinite break
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
          m1 = 0.9*m1 + 0.1*grad
          v2 = 0.999*v2 + 0.001*(grad*grad)
          b1=1.0 - 0.9**t
          b2=1.0 - 0.999**t
          step=m1/b1/(np.sqrt(v2/b2)+1e-8)
          # clip
          step=np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)
          h=project_feasible(h - lr*step, n)
          if stall > 2000:
             h=best_h.copy()
             m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0; stall=0
             lr *= 0.5
             if lr < 1e-4:
                 break
    return best_h,best_c
```
But resetting t=0 inside while after stall and lr reduce; if we set t=0, b1 etc. Good. But if no improvement from best for long, resetting to best may loop; reduce lr eventually break.

`beta_lr` maybe [(5,0.12),(20,0.08),(80,0.04),(300,0.015)]. For small time, maybe two.

Need be careful: `0.9**t` for t large underflows to 0, fine. Use math? pow. For t huge, 1.0.

Soft weights for beta very high: if β*(c-m) has many below -745 underflow; max zero, weights one at argmax. That's okay. But if multiple max, weights distributed.

def slsqp_refine(h,n,time_limit,beta=80,maxiter=200):
   if minimize is None or time_limit<15 or n>200: return h
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   for b,mi in [(beta,maxiter),(beta*5,max(50,maxiter//2))]:
      if time.time() > ...? can't inside but before.
      try:
         res=minimize(soft_objective, h, jac=soft_gradient, args=(n,b), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':mi,'ftol':1e-12})
         if res.x.size==n:
            h2=project_feasible(res.x,n); c2=compute_c5(h2,n); update
      except: pass
   return best_h

def binary_pal_initial(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    m=n//2
    k=n//4
    if k*2 != n//2? for n%4==0 yes.
    pairs=np.arange(m)
    rng.shuffle(pairs)
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    ones=pairs[:k]
    h[ones]=1; h[n-1-ones]=1
    return h

def binary_local_search(h,n,budget,seed):
    h=project_feasible(h.astype(float),n)
    pairval=(h[:n//2]>0.5).astype(int)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_mc=compute_c_values(best_h).max()
    t_end=time.time()+budget
    ones_list=[i for i,v in enumerate(pairval) if v]
    zeros_list=[i for i,v in enumerate(pairval) if not v]
    T=2.0
    scale_max=(n/2)*2? Let's use Δcount = (new_c-best_c)*n/2. Since c unit? best_mc. delta = new_mc - best_mc. T from 1.0 to 0.05. Accept if delta<0 or rng.random()<exp(-delta/T).
    it=0
    while time.time()<t_end-1 and ones_list and zeros_list:
       i=ones_list[rng.randint(len(ones_list))]
       j=zeros_list[rng.randint(len(zeros_list))]
       # attempt swap
       # set pair i 0, pair j 1
       old=...
       h[i]=0; h[n-1-i]=0; h[j]=1; h[n-1-j]=1
       new_mc=compute_c_values(h).max()
       diff=new_mc-best_mc
       if diff < 1e-12 or rng.random()<math.exp(-diff/T):
           accept
           pairval[i]=0; pairval[j]=1
           update lists
           best_mc=new_mc; best_h=h.copy()
       else:
           revert
       it +=1
       if it % 200 ==0:
           T=max(0.05, T*0.997)
    return best_h,best_mc*2/n
```

```
def interpolate_h(h_old,n_old,n_new):
   if n_old==n_new: return h_old.copy()
   x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*2.0/n_old
   x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
   h=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_old, left=h_old[0], right=h_old[-1])
   return project_feasible(h,n_new)
```

def try_candidate(h,n,best_h,best_c,best_n):
    if h is None or n<=0: return best_h,best_c,best_n
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    if h.shape[0]!=n: h=np.resize? no, if len mismatch return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return ...
    c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-15:
       return h.copy(), c, n
    if best_h is None:
       return h.copy(), c, n
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Binary local search details:
```
def binary_local_search(h, n, budget, seed):
    if budget <=0: return h, compute_c5(h,n)
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
    m=n//2
    if m%2 !=0 or n%4 !=0: # fallback: use general? We'll select n%4. If not, project.
    pairval=(h[:m] > 0.5).astype(int)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_mc=float(np.max(compute_c_values(best_h)))
    ones=[i for i,v in enumerate(pairval) if v==1]
    zeros=[i for i,v in enumerate(pairval) if v==0]
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    t_end=time.time()+budget
    T=1.5
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time.time() < t_end - 0.5 and len(ones)>0 and len(zeros)>0:
       it +=1
       i=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
       j=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
       # swap pair values i (1) ->0, j (0)->1
       h[i]=0.0; h[n-1-i]=0.0
       h[j]=1.0; h[n-1-j]=1.0
       mc=float(np.max(compute_c_values(h)))
       diff=mc-best_mc
       accept = diff < 1e-12
       if not accept and diff > 0:
          val = -diff / T
          if val > -50.0 and rng.random() < math.exp(val): accept=True
       if accept:
          pairval[i]=0; pairval[j]=1
          # update lists
          try:
             ones.remove(i); ones.append(j)
          except ValueError: pass
          try:
             zeros.remove(j); zeros.append(i)
          except ValueError: pass
          best_mc=mc; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
          # revert
          h[i]=1.0; h[n-1-i]=1.0
          h[j]=0.0; h[n-1-j]=0.0
          no_improve+=1
       if it % 100 ==0:
          T = max(0.03, T*0.995)
          if no_improve > 200:
             h=best_h.copy()
             pairval=(h[:m]>0.5).astype(int)
             ones=[i for i,v in enumerate(pairval) if v==1]
             zeros=[i for i,v in enumerate(pairval) if v==0]
             no_improve=0
             T=max(0.05,T*0.7)
    return best_h, float(best_mc)*(2.0/n)
```
But after revert, h is undamaged? We set i and j back to old: old i=1, j=0. Yes.
What if i and j same? impossible because one in ones, one zeros.
The lists may have duplicates? We append j to ones and i to zeros; remove i from ones but i was in ones; remove j from zeros. Good.

If no_improve reset to best, but pairval from best. Good.

def binary_pattern_code(n, code, seed):
    m=n//2
    p=[]; while len(p)<m: p.extend(code)
    p=np.array(p[:m],int)
    # adjust count: need k=n//4 ones.
    h=np.zeros(n)
    idx=range(m)
    # If sum p != k, adjust: if too many, set some 1 to 0 (randomize); if too few set 0 to 1
    k=n//4
    ones_idx=np.where(p==1)[0]; zeros_idx=np.where(p==0)[0]
    if len(ones_idx)>k: # turn excess to 0
       rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
       perm=rng.permutation(ones_idx)
       for t in perm[k:]:
          p[t]=0
    elif len(ones_idx)<k:
       rng...

       need=k-len(ones_idx)
       for t in perm[:need]: p[t]=1
    h[p==1]=1? Need assign positions: ones=np.where(p==1)[0]; h[ones]=1; h[n-1-ones]=1
    return project_feasible(h,n)

Let's design `binary_candidates`:
```
def binary_candidates(n,budget,seed):
    t_end = time.time()+budget
    best_h=None; best_c=1e9
    attempt=0
    while time.time() < t_end - 3:
       attempt +=1
       if attempt ==1:
          h = binary_pattern_code(n,[1,1,0,0],seed)
       elif attempt ==2:
          h = binary_pattern_code(n,[1,0,1,0],seed+1)
       elif attempt ==3:
          h = binary_pattern_code(n,[1,1,1,0,0,0],seed+2)
       else:
          h = binary_pal_initial(n, seed+attempt*101)
       search_time = min(25.0, t_end - time.time() - 1.0)
       if search_time < 5.0: break
       hh,cc = binary_local_search(h,n,search_time,seed+attempt)
       if cc < best_c:
          best_h=hh; best_c=cc
    if best_h is None:
       return None,1e9
    return best_h,best_c
```
Maybe search_time 25 total budget 80 => 3 attempts? Pattern first 25, second25, third25; good.
Budget could be 120; more attempts. okay.

`binary_pattern_code` with code length not multiple; `p` may not have density exactly half due truncation. adjust using rng. Need preserve pair count exact. We'll implement adjust as described. But if code has exact density half, truncation might slightly off. Adjust.

`binary_pattern_code`:
```
def binary_pattern_code(n, code, seed):
    m=n//2; k=n//4
    p=[]
    while len(p)<m: p.extend(code)
    p=np.array(p[:m],int)
    ones_idx=np.flatnonzero(p==1)
    zeros_idx=np.flatnonzero(p==0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    if len(ones_idx)>k:
       perm=rng.permutation(ones_idx)
       for t in perm[k:]: p[t]=0
    elif len(ones_idx)<k:
       need=k-len(ones_idx)
       if len(zeros_idx)>0:
          perm=rng.permutation(zeros_idx)
          for t in perm[:need]: p[t]=1
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    idx=np.flatnonzero(p==1)
    h[idx]=1.0; h[n-1-idx]=1.0
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If n not mul4, k floor? We only use mul4. If n%4=2, k=n//4 gives sum 2k=n/2-1; project adjust may fractional. Fine.

`binary_pal_initial`: if n%4 not0, target n/2 may not be integer pair sum; project makes fractional. But we use n mul4.

def make_linear(n):
 x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
 h=1.0 - 0.5*x
 return project_feasible(h,n)
```

def try_symmetries(h,n,best_h,best_c,best_n):
   if h is None or n<=0: return best_h,best_c,best_n
   v = 0.5*(h + h[::-1])
   best_h,best_c,best_n = try_candidate(v,n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
   v = 0.5*(h + (1.0 - h[::-1]))
   best_h,best_c,best_n = try_candidate(v,n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
   return best_h,best_c,best_n
```
Call after initial, after each major maybe. But if h from binary palindrome, v2 constant 0.5 (c=0.5) ignored. v1 same.

Top-level functions:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)  # maybe dangerous but avoids error? If size mismatch, resizing. But if initial length not n? n passed len. okay.
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(6):
       diff = h.sum() - target
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
       cnt = int(mask.sum())
       if cnt == 0: break
       h[mask] -= diff / cnt
       h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(h.sum() - target) > 1e-9:
       lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
       for _ in range(50):
          mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
          s=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
          if s > target: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid
       tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
       h=np.clip(h-tau,0.0,1.0)
       res=h.sum()-target
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
       cnt=int(mask.sum())
       if cnt>0 and abs(res)>1e-13:
          h[mask]-=res/cnt
          h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
Using `np.resize` if size mismatch can produce repeated; maybe not. If initial vector length inconsistent? n from len, so no. But if candidate creation bug, OK. However resizing might change sum and feasibility; project fixes. Fine.

`try_candidate`: if `h.shape[0] != n`, project resizes. Good.

def compute_c_values(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

`softmax_weights`: Need if beta is inf? We use finite. If beta<=0 uniform. If c contains NaN? no.
```
def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    l=c.size
    if beta <=0 or l ==0:
       return np.ones(l)/max(1,l)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    x=beta*(c-m)
    # x max 0, but due floats maybe >0 tiny; subtract max
    x=x - np.max(x)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros(l); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w
    return w/s
```

`weighted_gradient`:
```
def weighted_gradient(w,h,n):
    if n<=0: return np.array([])
    pref=np.zeros(2*n)
    np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:], dtype=float)? `np.cumsum` has out but dtype? Use `pref[1:] = np.cumsum(w)`
    V = pref[n:2*n] - pref[0:n]
    conv1 = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')
    S1 = conv1[n-1:n-1+n]
    wrev = w[::-1]
    conv2 = np.convolve(h, wrev, mode='full')
    S2 = conv2[n-1:n-1+n]
    return V - S1 - S2
```
`pref` length 2n, w length 2n-1, pref[1:] length 2n-1, okay. `V` length n. If w not length 2n-1, maybe? We pass.

`soft_objective`:
```
def soft_objective(h, n, beta):
   c=compute_c_values(h)
   m=float(np.max(c))
   if beta <=0: return m
   x=beta*(c-m)
   x=x - np.max(x)
   s=np.sum(np.exp(x))
   if s<=0: return m
   return float((m + np.log(s))/beta)
```
Actually if x max 0, logsumexp = m + log(s)/beta? Here x=beta*(c-m), max 0. logsumexp(beta*(c-m))=log(s). Smooth max unnormalized = m + (1/beta)log(s). Return that. This equals (m + log(s))/beta? Wait (m + log(s))/beta is wrong dimension: m is unnormalized, beta has units? We need return c-scale: `m + np.log(s)/beta`. Because c_max + (1/β) log∑e^{β(c-cmax)}. So:
```
return float(m + np.log(s)/beta)
```
Good catch. Also in smooth objective previously used (m+logsum)/beta; that's equivalent to m? Let's check: logsumexp(β c)=β m + log s. Divide by β => m + log s /β. So return m+log(s)/β. Use that.

`soft_gradient` returns gradient of this? Derivative of m+1/β log s = ∑ softmax w c_i' (same). No β factor. Good.

`hard_objective_unnorm`: return max c.

`slsqp_refine`: If `minimize is None`. Use list bounds. `options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12}`. For equality constraints with bounds, SLSQP may struggle if initial exactly constraint. Good.

```
def slsqp_refine(h,n,time_limit,beta=80):
    if minimize is None or n<=0 or time_limit <=10: return h
    if n > 180: return h
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    maxiter1 = 150 if n <=100 else (80 if n <=140 else 40)
    if time_limit < 30: maxiter1 = max(20, maxiter1//4)
    elif time_limit < 60: maxiter1 = max(40, maxiter1//2)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    attempts = [(beta,maxiter1), (beta*4.0, max(20, maxiter1//2))]
    for b,mi in attempts:
       try:
          res = minimize(soft_objective, h, jac=soft_gradient, args=(n,b), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':mi, 'ftol':1e-12})
          if res.x is not None and len(res.x)==n:
             h2=project_feasible(res.x,n)
             c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
             if c2 < best_c:
                best_h=h2; best_c=c2
                h=h2.copy()
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best_h
```
If first attempt worsens, continue from h (not best?) We update h only if improved; if not, h remains original? But SLSQP may produce worse; we don't set h to res.x if worse. Good. If first improves, use as start for second. If first warning but not better, still h original. Good.

`adam_refine`: Need objective c5 unnormalized? We compute `hard = float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))`. Good. In gradient, `w=softmax_weights(c,beta)`, `grad=weighted_gradient(w,h,n)`. This gradient is derivative of unnormalized max; scaling by dx not needed. Good.

Use factor `lr_scale = min(1.0, math.sqrt(100.0/float(n)))` if n>0.

```
def adam_refine(h,n,time_limit,beta_lr):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if time_limit<=0 or n<=0: return best_h,best_c
    t_end=time.time()+time_limit
    lr_scale=1.0
    if n>100: lr_scale=math.sqrt(100.0/float(n))
    while time.time()<t_end-1 and not collections? Just loop beta_lr.
    for beta,lr in beta_lr:
       if time.time()>t_end-1: break
       m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0; stall=0; lr_eff=lr*lr_scale
       while t<1000000 and time.time()<t_end-0.5:
          t +=1
          c=compute_c_values(h)
          hard=float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
          if hard < best_c - 1e-13: ...
          else: stall +=1
          w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
          grad=weighted_gradient(w,h,n)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): reset? break
          m1=0.9*m1+0.1*grad
          v2=0.999*v2+0.001*grad*grad
          b1=1.0 - 0.9**t
          b2=1.0 - 0.999**t
          step = (m1/b1) / (np.sqrt(v2/b2)+1e-8)
          step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)
          h = project_feasible(h - lr_eff*step, n)
          if stall > 5000 or hard > best_c + 0.01:
              h=best_h.copy(); m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0; stall=0; lr_eff*=0.5
              if lr_eff < 1e-4: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
Potential: if hard > best_c+0.01, reset quickly. For target 0.38, 0.01 absolute maybe large; allow. Maybe use 0.003? I'll use 0.005. If search exploring, could reset too often. Maybe only stall > 3000. Use no hard-reset based on increase; let Adam recover. But if it wanders, time wasted. Use increase threshold 0.01. Fine.

One more important thought: The above Adam uses unnormalized gradient. But if c values large (n=400, cmax~95), beta=300 gives exponent up to 0; softmax weights. The smooth objective m+log(s)/beta; gradient with weights. If beta high, gradient from one active. Good.

Should we set beta values relative to n? cmax~n/8? If beta fixed 300, for n=400 cmax~96, exponent range 28800, one active. Good; for n=100 cmax~24, exponent range 34800, one active. If multiple active, okay. If beta=5, width c=0.2 -> C width 0.0004 for n=100? Actually C width c_n? c width 0.2/dx=0.2/(0.02)=10 in C? Wait C=c*2/n; c width 0.2, C width 0.004 for n100. Good. beta=5 maybe smooth over all lags with c within ~0.2? The c values range 0..20; exp weights span 0.2/5=0.04? Actually softmax βc; weights for c difference Δc=0.2 => exp(-1)=0.37; for Δc=1 => exp(-5)=0.0067. So β=5 focuses lags within ~0.5c (C~0.005). Good. β=1 focuses all; better maybe start β=2. Use 5 okay.

Maybe first β=2 to explore. But parent already close. Use [(2,0.15),(10,0.1),(50,0.05),(200,0.02)]? For beta=2 width c~0.5. Let's adjust beta_lr to (2,0.15) first. But logsum with β=2 may be too smooth and not reduce max quickly. Could do (5, 10, 50, 200). Fine.

Now, in `adam_refine`, `if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad))`: We currently break beta loop but not outer. Add `if not finite: h=best_h.copy(); break` then outer continues maybe. Simpler:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
    h = best_h.copy(); m1=...; break
```
No need.

Potential performance: `np.all(np.isfinite(grad))` each iter extra. Could skip; h finite, grad finite. Remove to save. If h becomes nan? project clip no. Remove finite check.

Use `np.exp(x)` with x ~ -700 underflows fine; if x max 0. Good.

Now, test syntax in head for `slsqp_refine`: `if minimize is None` but minimize could be function; okay. If import failed, variable `minimize` exists None. If import succeeded, `minimize` function. Good.

Now, if `n` is float? We pass int. In interpol, nnew int. Good.

Let's maybe add `if best_n <=0` before final. If best_h None after all? We set linear.

Now, code. Ensure final fence. Need no extra text. We'll output code block.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(6):
        diff = h.sum() - target
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        cnt = int(mask.sum())
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        h[mask] -= diff / cnt
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    if abs(h.sum() - target) > 1e-9:
        lo = -1.0
        hi = 1.0
        for _ in range(55):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        h = np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
        res = h.sum() - target
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        cnt = int(mask.sum())
        if cnt > 0 and abs(res) > 1e-13:
            h[mask] -= res / cnt
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def compute_c_values(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def compute_c5(h, n):
    c = compute_c_values(h)
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    m = c.size
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    x = beta * (c - cm)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def weighted_gradient(w, h, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)

    pref = np.zeros(2 * n, dtype=float)
    pref[1:] = np.cumsum(w)
    V = pref[n:2 * n] - pref[0:n]

    conv1 = np.convolve(h, w, mode="full")
    s1 = conv1[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    wrev = w[::-1]
    conv2 = np.convolve(h, wrev, mode="full")
    s2 = conv2[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    return V - s1 - s2


def sum_eq_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)


def sum_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def soft_objective(h, n, beta):
    c = compute_c_values(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return m
    x = beta * (c - m)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(x)))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return float(m + math.log(s) / beta)


def soft_gradient(h, n, beta):
    c = compute_c_values(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    w = softmax_weights(c, beta)
    return weighted_gradient(w, h, n)


def hard_objective_unnorm(h, n):
    c = compute_c_values(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    return float(np.max(c))


def hard_gradient(h, n):
    c = compute_c_values(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    idx = int(np.argmax(c))
    w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    w[idx] = 1.0
    return weighted_gradient(w, h, n)


def seconds_left(t0, total):
    return float(total) - (time.time() - t0)


def try_candidate(h, n, best_h, best_c, best_n):
    if h is None or n <= 0:
        return best_h, best_c, best_n
    try:
        h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), int(n))
        if h.size != n:
            return best_h, best_c, best_n
        c = compute_c5(h, n)
        if math.isfinite(c):
            if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                return h.copy(), float(c), int(n)
            if best_h is None:
                return h.copy(), float(c), int(n)
    except Exception:
        return best_h, best_c, best_n
    return best_h, best_c, best_n


def try_symmetries(h, n, best_h, best_c, best_n):
    if h is None or n <= 0:
        return best_h, best_c, best_n
    try:
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
        v1 = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(v1, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
        v2 = 0.5 * (h + (1.0 - h[::-1]))
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(v2, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_h, best_c, best_n


def make_linear(n):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 1.0 - 0.5 * x
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def interpolate_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    h_old = np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float)
    if n_old <= 0 or n_new <= 0:
        return make_linear(n_new)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h_old, n_new)
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old, left=float(h_old[0]), right=float(h_old[-1]))
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)


def perturb_h(h, n, scale, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return project_feasible(h + rng.randn(n) * scale, n)


def adam_refine(h, n, time_limit, beta_lr):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if time_limit <= 0.5 or n <= 0:
        return best_h, best_c

    t_end = time.time() + time_limit
    lr_scale = 1.0
    if n > 100:
        lr_scale = math.sqrt(100.0 / float(n))
        if lr_scale < 0.25:
            lr_scale = 0.25

    for beta, lr in beta_lr:
        if time.time() > t_end - 1.0:
            break
        m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        v2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        t = 0
        stall = 0
        lr_eff = lr * lr_scale

        while t < 1000000 and time.time() < t_end - 0.5:
            t += 1
            c = compute_c_values(h)
            hard = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))

            if hard < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c = hard
                best_h = h.copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            w = softmax_weights(c, beta)
            grad = weighted_gradient(w, h, n)

            m1 = 0.9 * m1 + 0.1 * grad
            v2 = 0.999 * v2 + 0.001 * (grad * grad)

            b1 = 1.0 - 0.9 ** t
            b2 = 1.0 - 0.999 ** t
            step = (m1 / b1) / (np.sqrt(v2 / b2) + 1e-8)
            step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)
            h = project_feasible(h - lr_eff * step, n)

            if stall > 4000 or (t > 100 and hard > best_c + 0.01):
                h = best_h.copy()
                m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
                v2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
                t = 0
                stall = 0
                lr_eff *= 0.5
                if lr_eff < 1e-4:
                    break

    return best_h, best_c


def slsqp_refine(h, n, time_limit, beta=80.0):
    if minimize is None or n <= 0 or time_limit <= 12.0:
        return h
    if n > 180:
        return h

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    if n <= 100:
        mi1 = 160
    elif n <= 140:
        mi1 = 90
    else:
        mi1 = 45

    if time_limit < 30.0:
        mi1 = max(20, mi1 // 4)
    elif time_limit < 60.0:
        mi1 = max(40, mi1 // 2)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_eq_fun, 'jac': sum_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]

    attempts = [
        (float(beta), max(20, mi1)),
        (float(beta) * 4.0, max(15, mi1 // 2)),
    ]

    current = best_h.copy()
    for b, mi in attempts:
        try:
            res = minimize(
                soft_objective,
                current,
                jac=soft_gradient,
                args=(n, b),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": mi, "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
            if res.x is not None and len(res.x) == n:
                h2 = project_feasible(res.x, n)
                c2 = compute_c5(h2, n)
                if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
                    best_h = h2
                    best_c = c2
                    current = h2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h


def binary_pattern_code(n, code, seed):
    m = n // 2
    k = n // 4
    p = []
    while len(p) < m:
        p.extend(code)
    p = np.array(p[:m], dtype=int)

    ones_idx = np.flatnonzero(p == 1)
    zeros_idx = np.flatnonzero(p == 0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    if len(ones_idx) > k:
        perm = rng.permutation(ones_idx)
        for t in perm[k:]:
            p[t] = 0
    elif len(ones_idx) < k:
        need = k - len(ones_idx)
        if len(zeros_idx) > 0:
            perm = rng.permutation(zeros_idx)
            for t in perm[:need]:
                p[t] = 1

    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(p == 1)
    if idx.size:
        h[idx] = 1.0
        h[n - 1 - idx] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def binary_pal_initial(n, seed):
    m = n // 2
    k = n // 4
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    pairs = np.arange(m, dtype=int)
    rng.shuffle(pairs)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    ones = pairs[:k]
    h[ones] = 1.0
    h[n - 1 - ones] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def binary_local_search(h, n, budget, seed):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_mc = float(np.max(compute_c_values(best_h)))
    best_c = best_mc * (2.0 / n)

    if budget <= 0.5:
        return best_h, best_c

    m = n // 2
    pairval = (h[:m] > 0.5).astype(int)
    ones = [i for i, v in enumerate(pairval) if v == 1]
    zeros = [i for i, v in enumerate(pairval) if v == 0]

    if not ones or not zeros:
        return best_h, best_c

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    t_end = time.time() + budget
    T = 1.2
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.time() < t_end - 0.5 and len(ones) > 0 and len(zeros) > 0:
        it += 1
        i = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
        j = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]

        h[i] = 0.0
        h[n - 1 - i] = 0.0
        h[j] = 1.0
        h[n - 1 - j] = 1.0

        mc = float(np.max(compute_c_values(h)))
        diff = mc - best_mc
        accept = diff < 1e-12
        if not accept and diff > 0.0:
            val = -diff / max(T, 1e-9)
            if val > -50.0 and rng.random() < math.exp(val):
                accept = True

        if accept:
            pairval[i] = 0
            pairval[j] = 1
            try:
                ones.remove(i)
                ones.append(j)
            except Exception:
                ones = [x for x in range(m) if pairval[x] == 1]
                zeros = [x for x in range(m) if pairval[x] == 0]
            try:
                zeros.remove(j)
                zeros.append(i)
            except Exception:
                ones = [x for x in range(m) if pairval[x] == 1]
                zeros = [x for x in range(m) if pairval[x] == 0]
            best_mc = mc
            best_h = h.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            h[i] = 1.0
            h[n - 1 - i] = 1.0
            h[j] = 0.0
            h[n - 1 - j] = 0.0
            no_improve += 1

        if it % 80 == 0:
            T = max(0.03, T * 0.993)
        if no_improve > 250:
            h = best_h.copy()
            pairval = (h[:m] > 0.5).astype(int)
            ones = [x for x in range(m) if pairval[x] == 1]
            zeros = [x for x in range(m) if pairval[x] == 0]
            no_improve = 0
            T = max(0.05, T * 0.65)

    return best_h, float(best_mc) * (2.0 / n)


def binary_candidates(n, budget, seed):
    t_end = time.time() + budget
    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e9
    attempt = 0

    while time.time() < t_end - 3.0:
        attempt += 1
        if attempt == 1:
            h = binary_pattern_code(n, [1, 1, 0, 0], seed)
        elif attempt == 2:
            h = binary_pattern_code(n, [1, 0, 1, 0], seed + 1)
        elif attempt == 3:
            h = binary_pattern_code(n, [1, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0], seed + 2)
        else:
            h = binary_pal_initial(n, seed + 101 * attempt)

        sl = min(25.0, t_end - time.time() - 1.0)
        if sl < 5.0:
            break

        hh, cc = binary_local_search(h, n, sl, seed + attempt)
        if math.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_c = cc

    if best_h is None:
        return None, 1e9
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(budget_s)

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e9
    best_n = 0

    try:
        iv = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if iv.size > 0:
            n0 = int(iv.size)
            h0 = project_feasible(iv, n0)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(h0, n0, best_h, best_c, best_n)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None:
        h0 = make_linear(100)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(h0, 100, best_h, best_c, best_n)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if best_h is not None and seconds_left(t0, total) > 170:
        sl = min(95.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 130)
        if sl > 20.0:
            hh, hc = adam_refine(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                sl,
                [(5.0, 0.12), (20.0, 0.08), (80.0, 0.04), (300.0, 0.02)],
            )
            best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if best_h is not None and seconds_left(t0, total) > 90 and best_n <= 150:
        sl = min(45.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 80)
        if sl > 12.0:
            hh = slsqp_refine(best_h, best_n, sl, beta=80.0)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    binary_ns = [100, 120, 160, 200]
    for nbin in binary_ns:
        if seconds_left(t0, total) < 170:
            break
        sl = min(45.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 150)
        if sl < 15.0:
            break
        bh, bc = binary_candidates(int(nbin), sl, int(seed) + int(nbin))
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(bh, int(nbin), best_h, best_c, best_n)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

        if (
            bh is not None
            and math.isfinite(bc)
            and bc < best_c + 0.02
            and seconds_left(t0, total) > 130
        ):
            sl2 = min(30.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 120)
            if sl2 > 10.0:
                hh, hc = adam_refine(
                    bh,
                    int(nbin),
                    sl2,
                    [(20.0, 0.08), (80.0, 0.04), (300.0, 0.02)],
                )
                best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh, int(nbin), best_h, best_c, best_n)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if best_n < 100:
        alt_ns = [100, 120, 160, 200, 300]
    elif best_n < 160:
        alt_ns = [120, 200, 300, 400]
    else:
        alt_ns = [100, 200]

    seen = set()
    for nnew in alt_ns:
        nnew = int(nnew)
        if nnew in seen or nnew == best_n:
            continue
        seen.add(nnew)
        if seconds_left(t0, total) < 140:
            break
        sl = min(42.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 130)
        if sl < 15.0:
            break

        hnew = interpolate_h(best_h, best_n, nnew)
        hh, hc = adam_refine(
            hnew,
            nnew,
            sl,
            [(10.0, 0.10), (50.0, 0.05), (200.0, 0.02)],
        )
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh, nnew, best_h, best_c, best_n)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

        if nnew <= 130 and seconds_left(t0, total) > 100:
            sl2 = min(20.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 90)
            if sl2 > 8.0:
                hh2 = slsqp_refine(best_h, best_n, sl2, beta=100.0)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh2, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    pert_scales = [0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15]
    for i, sc in enumerate(pert_scales):
        if seconds_left(t0, total) < 100:
            break
        sl = min(22.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 90)
        if sl < 8.0:
            break
        hp = perturb_h(best_h, best_n, sc, int(seed) + 5000 + i)
        hh, hc = adam_refine(
            hp,
            best_n,
            sl,
            [(20.0, 0.05), (100.0, 0.03), (400.0, 0.01)],
        )
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = try_symmetries(best_h, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if seconds_left(t0, total) > 60 and best_h is not None:
        if best_n <= 130:
            sl = min(22.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 30)
            if sl > 6.0:
                hh = slsqp_refine(best_h, best_n, sl, beta=150.0)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
        else:
            sl = min(15.0, seconds_left(t0, total) - 15)
            if sl > 4.0:
                hh, hc = adam_refine(
                    best_h,
                    best_n,
                    sl,
                    [(500.0, 0.01), (1000.0, 0.005)],
                )
                best_h, best_c, best_n = try_candidate(hh, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    try:
        val = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h, best_n)
        if val is not None:
            val = float(val)
            if math.isfinite(val) and val >= 0.0 and (best_c - val) < 1e-6:
                best_c = min(best_c, val)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n))
```
